focus_tree = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_focus

	# One lifecycle tree follows the country object through prewar WRK/VAD,
	# the temporary WKR/VAD/TVA claimants, and the reunified WRK route. Keeping
	# all stages in one tree avoids relying on a same-tick focus-tree swap.
	#
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# LAYOUT CONTRACT
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# Exactly nine focuses carry absolute x/y - one anchor per lifecycle
	# block. Every other focus is placed with relative_position_id pointing
	# at a focus that is genuinely one of its prerequisites, so a whole
	# branch moves as a unit when its head moves and no arrow is ever drawn
	# upwards.
	#
	# The vertical pitch is one. A straight chain is x = 0, y = 1 and a fork
	# is x = -1 / x = +1 (or -2 / +2 where a side carries a wider subtree)
	# on the same row, so a mutually exclusive pair reads as a symmetric V
	# under its parent. This matches the reference mod, where a drop of one
	# row accounts for 87% of all prerequisite links and a drop of two for
	# 12%; here the figures are 91% and 5%.
	#
	# A drop of more than one row is not decoration and is never applied
	# uniformly. Clausewitz draws a prerequisite link as a step: down out of
	# the parent, horizontally at the vertical midpoint between the two
	# rows, then down into the child. An odd drop puts that horizontal run
	# in the gap between two rows, where it crosses nothing. An even drop
	# puts it through the centre of row py + dy/2, so that row has to be
	# clear across the columns the run spans.
	#
	# Every drop of 2 or 3 in this file therefore exists to keep one
	# specific horizontal run off an occupied row, and the validator pins
	# each one. The convergence node TVA_print_interchangeable_repair_modules
	# is the single exception: it takes six prerequisites split across two
	# adjacent rows, so no drop can be odd for both halves, and its link
	# from TVA_network_observation_posts passes over TVA_seal_the_approaches.
	#
	# Bands are disjoint in y between lifecycle phases and disjoint in x
	# within a phase. Only one band is ever visible at a time, but keeping
	# them disjoint means no two focuses in this file resolve to the same
	# grid cell, which makes an accidental overlap a validator failure
	# rather than something a player discovers.
	#
	#   band                  x range   y range   anchor
	#   prewar WRK              0..4      0..5    WRK_measure_confederation_fault_lines (1,0)
	#   prewar VAD             10..14     0..5    VAD_review_district_obligations (11,0)
	#   retired stubs          20..22     0..1    ADISCORD_vorkerland_stabilize_claimant_regime (21,0)
	#   wartime WKR             0..23     7..17   WKR_affirm_worker_mandate (8,7)
	#   wartime VAD            26..54     7..19   VAD_proclaim_joint_charter (33,7)
	#   wartime TVA            56..83     7..19   TVA_codify_utilitarian_directorate (68,7)
	#   postwar Worker          0..4     21..27   WRK_worker_convene_reunification_congress (2,21)
	#   postwar Joint          12..16    21..28   WRK_joint_convene_restoration_council (14,21)
	#   postwar Worx           24..28    21..28   WRK_utilitarian_form_reconstruction_directorate (25,21)
	#
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# CLAIMANT DEPTH BRANCHES
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# Each claimant carries seven depth modules in the columns its wartime
	# band reserved for them. They attach to trunk focuses that already
	# exist, so none of them reflows the spine.
	#
	#   module        WKR x / y      VAD x / y       TVA x / y
	#   war economy   19..21 / 10..11  52..54 / 11..12  81..83 / 10..11
	#   doctrine       0..4  / 14..15  30..44 / 14..15  56..58 / 11..12
	#   late war      21..23 / 13..14  33..35 / 13..14  73..75 / 14..15
	#   legitimacy    11..13 / 12..13  47..49 /  8..9   69..71 / 12..13
	#   diplomacy      5..7  / 10      51..53 /  8      76..78 / 11
	#   air           16..18 /  8..9   41..43 /  8..9   62..64 /  8..9
	#   foreign       21..23 /  8..9   27..29 /  8..9   69..71 /  8..9
	#
	# War economy, late war, legitimacy and diplomacy are shared by both of
	# a claimant's variants; doctrine is not. The doctrine module hangs off
	# each variant's settlement focus and is the reason an Anton AI and a
	# Nikita AI field different armies rather than the same army with a
	# different name.
	#
	# The late-war module becomes available only in the central showdown.
	# That phase gate lives in available, never in allow_branch, so the
	# branch stays visible and legible before it opens. The legitimacy module goes through
	# ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy and never writes the ledger.
	# Diplomacy is the module a competent player may reasonably skip.
	#
	# Every focus also carries search_filters so the in-game focus search
	# box can group the tree by what a focus actually pays out. The bands,
	# the anchors and the one-focus-per-cell rule are all enforced by
	# tools/validators/validate_adiscord_vorkerland_civil_war_focus.py.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			OR = {
				tag = WRK
				tag = WKR
				tag = VAD
				tag = TVA
			}
		}
	}
	default = no

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# Prewar WRK: a short preparation block before the automatic collapse.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = WRK_measure_confederation_fault_lines
		icon = GFX_focus_WRK_measure_confederation_fault_lines
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
			FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		}
		x = 1
		y = 0
		cost = 5
		allow_branch = { tag = WRK has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar }
		available = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar }
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		completion_reward = {
			add_political_power = 10
			add_stability = 0.01
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wrk_fault_lines_measured
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_preparations_carry_over_tt
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 12 }
	}

	focus = {
		id = WRK_convene_council_of_republics

		x = -1

	# Worker and Vadl can still attempt a narrow emergency compact before the
	# collapse. The alternative preserves the same time window but spends it on
	# reserves and district authority. Course flags are written on selection so
	# the two countries can react to one another without a polling event.
	focus = {
		id = WRK_open_worker_vadl_backchannel
		icon = GFX_focus_WRK_open_worker_vadl_backchannel

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# WKR variant depth: two incompatible answers to "who is the republic".
	# The collapse roll picks the branch, so the two are never both visible;
	# the mutually_exclusive pair only exists so the tree can never be walked
	# down both political settlements if a later save-game edit shows both.
	#
	# Nikita line (ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists).
	# Personalist continuation of the revolution. Play pattern: sell stability
	# and factory output to buy war support, conscription and offensive tempo,
	# then convert the leader's surviving person into permanent emergency
	# powers. Fast, brittle, and diplomatically loud - it opens the volunteer
	# channel to the free republics instead of trading for recognition.
	#
	# Anton line (NOT ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists).
	# Managerial legitimacy. Play pattern: deliver output and rations first,
	# buy stability and construction instead of tempo, accept a slower and
	# flatter front in exchange for a home front that does not crack, and
	# settle authority on published norms rather than on a person. Its
	# diplomacy is technical exchange, not volunteers.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = WKR_declare_the_revolution_unfinished
		icon = GFX_focus_WKR_declare_the_revolution_unfinished
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_WAR_SUPPORT
			FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
			FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		}
		x = -1
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = WKR_publish_emergency_constitution
		cost = 2
		prerequisite = { focus = WKR_publish_emergency_constitution }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = WKR_govern_by_delivered_results }
		allow_branch = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# VAD variant depth: whose name the restoration is carried out in.
	#
	# Imperial line (NOT ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed).
	# Vlad Petrichev restores a hierarchy. Play pattern: hoard political power
	# and spend it on chancery decrees, buy officer quality and planning rather
	# than bodies, keep conscription low and the staff excellent, and pay for
	# it with a permanently unhappy rear. Diplomacy is recognition-seeking -
	# cheap war goals and better standing with anyone who prefers a legitimist
	# Vorkerland to a revolutionary one.
	#
	# Joint line (ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed, the rare roll).
	# The rescued chairman and the district guard govern together. Play
	# pattern: trade command quality and political power for reach - broad
	# conscription, high stability, a surrender limit that makes the state very
	# hard to knock over, and slow, wide, resilient offensives. Diplomacy runs
	# through the worker districts rather than through the crown.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# TVA variant depth: the directorate has no succession crisis, so its two
	# variants are a declared policy rather than a roll. Worx must publish one
	# of two answers to the same technocratic question - what is the scarce
	# resource that the war has to be optimised around. The two answers are
	# mutually exclusive and are read by the AI plans below.
	#
	# Continuous mobilisation (ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_throughput).
	# The scarce resource is time. Play pattern: convert the civil grid into
	# war output, run the population as a labour input, take the stability and
	# research penalties, and win before the machine wears out. Heavy industry
	# and line-change throughput, poor casualty economy.
	#
	# Preservation protocol (ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_preservation).
	# The scarce resource is trained people. Play pattern: evacuate the design
	# bureaus, refuse to spend specialists, hold ground cheaply and out-research
	# the war. Better defence, retention and research, materially less capacity
	# to attack. The two lines produce visibly different armies by the central
	# showdown.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = TVA_declare_continuous_mobilization
		icon = GFX_focus_TVA_declare_continuous_mobilization
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_WAR_SUPPORT
			FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
			FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		}
		x = -12
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = TVA_codify_utilitarian_directorate
		cost = 2
		prerequisite = { focus = TVA_codify_utilitarian_directorate }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = TVA_enact_the_preservation_protocol }
		allow_branch = {
			tag = TVA
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_government = technocracy
			has_country_leader = { character = TVA_Dorian_Worx ruling_only = yes }
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		bypass = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_continuous_mobilization }
		completion_reward = {
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_continuous_mobilization
			add_war_support = 0.03
			add_stability = -0.03
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_continuous_mobilization
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_throughput
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_variant_declaration = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_doctrine_dynamic_state = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_preservation }
			modifier = { factor = 2 surrender_progress > 0.10 }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_war = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_stability < 0.40 }
		}
	}

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	# Worker war economy. The soviet decides whether the districts eat or
	# the line runs. Stripping the civil economy buys factory output and

	# TVA diplomacy: the bracket winners and the outside powers.
	#
	# The directorate trades knowledge, not volunteers. It can open a
	# technical exchange abroad or hire a bracket winner outright. A
	# directorate that is out-researching everyone can skip this.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = TVA_open_the_technical_exchange
		icon = GFX_focus_TVA_open_the_technical_exchange
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_INTERNATIONAL_TRADE
			FOCUS_FILTER_RESEARCH
		}
		x = 2
		y = 3
		relative_position_id = TVA_publish_operational_metrics
		cost = 3
		prerequisite = { focus = TVA_publish_operational_metrics }
		mutually_exclusive = { focus = TVA_hire_the_bracket_victors }
		allow_branch = {
			tag = TVA
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		available = {
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		bypass = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_open_the_technical_exchange }
		completion_reward = {
			add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_technical_exchange days = 70 }
			add_political_power = 10
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.06 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_tva_open_the_technical_exchange
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_stability < 0.40 }
		}
	}

	focus = {
		id = TVA_hire_the_bracket_victors
		icon = GFX_focus_TVA_hire_the_bracket_victors
		search_filters = {
